  - Helpers de R1 en `common/time/business-time.ts` (firmas exactas de "Domain rules › R1").
  - `type Role = 'client' | 'staff'`; `interface AuthUser { id: string; email: string; name: string; role: Role }`; `interface RequestWithUser extends Request { user?: AuthUser }`; `Public()` + `IS_PUBLIC_KEY`; `Roles(...roles: Role[])` + `ROLES_KEY`; `CurrentUser()` (param decorator que devuelve `AuthUser | undefined`).
  - `JobRunner.run(job: string, fn: () => Promise<Record<string, number>>, options?: { force?: boolean }): Promise<{ ran: boolean; result: Record<string, number> }>`; `CommonModule` (`@Global()`, exporta `JobRunner`).
  - Configs: `appConfig` (`{ port, webUrl, apiPublicUrl, nodeEnv }`), `authConfig` (`{ secret, cookieDomain: string | null, google: { clientId, clientSecret } | null }`), `mercadoPagoConfig` (`{ accessToken: string | null, webhookSecret: string | null, simulatorEnabled: boolean }`), `schedulerConfig` (`{ enabled: boolean }`).
  - `configureApp(app: NestExpressApplication): void` en `src/app.setup.ts`; `main.ts` crea la app con `{ bodyParser: false }` y llama `configureApp`.
  - `createTestApp(): Promise<{ app: NestExpressApplication; dataSource: DataSource }>` en `test/utils/app.ts`.
  - `GET /api/health` (`@Public()`).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. Toda respuesta de error de la API tiene la forma `ApiErrorBody`; un error desconocido devuelve 500 `INTERNAL_ERROR` sin stack ni mensaje interno, y se loguea con stack del lado del servidor.
2. Un DTO inválido devuelve 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` con `details[].field` y `details[].messages`; un campo no declarado también es rechazado.
3. Los helpers de tiempo pasan los casos de borde de Montevideo (turno 21:00 local = día local; 23:30 local; fin de mes en `addMonths`; primer lunes).
4. `JobRunner.run` no ejecuta dos veces en paralelo el mismo job (lock de Postgres) y no ejecuta con `SCHEDULER_ENABLED=false` salvo `force`.
5. La API no arranca si falta `BETTER_AUTH_SECRET` (o tiene menos de 32 caracteres), o si en `production` faltan `MP_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `MP_WEBHOOK_SECRET`, `BREVO_API_KEY` o `BREVO_SENDER_EMAIL`. Valores vacíos (`KEY=`) cuentan como ausentes. `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID` sin `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET` (o al revés) es inválido.
6. `GET /api/health` responde `{ status: 'ok' }` sin sesión.

```ts
import {
  addDays, addMonths, businessDateTime, businessDayEnd, businessDayStart, businessMonth, businessTime,
  businessToday, diffDays, isFirstMondayOfMonth, isoWeekday, toBusinessDate,
} from './business-time.js';

describe('business-time (America/Montevideo, UTC-3)', () => {
  it('maps late-evening local instants to the local date', () => {
    expect(toBusinessDate(new Date('2026-11-16T00:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-11-15'); // 21:00 local
    expect(toBusinessDate(new Date('2026-11-16T02:30:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-11-15'); // 23:30 local
    expect(toBusinessDate(new Date('2026-11-16T03:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-11-16'); // 00:00 local
  });

  it('computes today in Montevideo', () => {
    expect(businessToday(new Date('2026-10-13T02:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10-12');
  });

  it('builds instants from local date and time', () => {
    expect(businessDateTime('2026-10-12', '19:00').toISOString()).toBe('2026-10-12T22:00:00.000Z');
    expect(businessDayStart('2026-10-12').toISOString()).toBe('2026-10-12T03:00:00.000Z');
    expect(businessDayEnd('2026-10-12').toISOString()).toBe('2026-10-13T02:59:59.999Z');
    expect(businessTime(new Date('2026-10-12T22:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('19:00');
  });

  it('does calendar math on business dates', () => {
    expect(addDays('2026-10-31', 1)).toBe('2026-11-01');
    expect(addMonths('2026-08-15', 3)).toBe('2026-11-15');
    expect(addMonths('2027-01-31', 1)).toBe('2027-02-28');
    expect(diffDays('2026-10-12', '2026-11-15')).toBe(34);
    expect(diffDays('2026-11-15', '2026-11-15')).toBe(0);
    expect(isoWeekday('2026-10-12')).toBe(1);
    expect(isoWeekday('2026-10-18')).toBe(7);
    expect(businessMonth('2026-10-12')).toBe('2026-10');
  });

  it('detects the first Monday of the month', () => {
    expect(isFirstMondayOfMonth('2026-10-05')).toBe(true);
    expect(isFirstMondayOfMonth('2026-10-12')).toBe(false);
    expect(isFirstMondayOfMonth('2026-11-02')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementar `business-time.ts`**

Usar `TZDate` de `@date-fns/tz` y `format` de `date-fns` para lo que depende de zona; la aritmética de fechas calendario se hace sobre `Date.UTC` para no depender de la zona del proceso:

```ts
import { TZDate } from '@date-fns/tz';
import { format } from 'date-fns';

export const BUSINESS_TZ = 'America/Montevideo';
const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;

const parseDate = (date: string): Date => {
  const [y, m, d] = date.split('-').map(Number);
  return new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
};
const formatDate = (value: Date): string => value.toISOString().slice(0, 10);

export function toBusinessDate(instant: Date): string {
  return format(new TZDate(instant.getTime(), BUSINESS_TZ), 'yyyy-MM-dd');
}

export function businessToday(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  return toBusinessDate(now);
}

export function businessDateTime(date: string, time: string): Date {
  const [y, m, d] = date.split('-').map(Number);
  const [hh, mm, ss = 0] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  return new Date(new TZDate(y, m - 1, d, hh, mm, ss, BUSINESS_TZ).getTime());
}

export function businessDayStart(date: string): Date {
  return businessDateTime(date, '00:00');
}

export function businessDayEnd(date: string): Date {
  return new Date(businessDayStart(addDays(date, 1)).getTime() - 1);
}

export function addDays(date: string, days: number): string {
  return formatDate(new Date(parseDate(date).getTime() + days * DAY_MS));
}

export function addMonths(date: string, months: number): string {
  const base = parseDate(date);
  const target = new Date(Date.UTC(base.getUTCFullYear(), base.getUTCMonth() + months, 1));
  const lastDay = new Date(Date.UTC(target.getUTCFullYear(), target.getUTCMonth() + 1, 0)).getUTCDate();
  target.setUTCDate(Math.min(base.getUTCDate(), lastDay));
  return formatDate(target);
}

export function diffDays(from: string, to: string): number {
  return Math.round((parseDate(to).getTime() - parseDate(from).getTime()) / DAY_MS);
}

export function isoWeekday(date: string): number {
  const day = parseDate(date).getUTCDay();
  return day === 0 ? 7 : day;
}

export function businessTime(instant: Date): string {
  return format(new TZDate(instant.getTime(), BUSINESS_TZ), 'HH:mm');
}

export function businessMonth(date: string): string {
  return date.slice(0, 7);
}

export function isFirstMondayOfMonth(date: string): boolean {
  return isoWeekday(date) === 1 && Number(date.slice(8, 10)) <= 7;
}
```

`apps/api/src/common/errors/all-exceptions.filter.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException, NotFoundException, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ValidationError } from 'class-validator';
import { AppError } from './app-error.js';
import { toErrorBody, validationExceptionFactory } from './all-exceptions.filter.js';

describe('toErrorBody', () => {
  it('keeps AppError bodies', () => {
    expect(toErrorBody(new AppError('SLOT_FULL'))).toEqual({
      statusCode: 409, code: 'SLOT_FULL', message: 'El turno está completo.',
    });
  });

  it('maps framework HTTP errors to generic codes without leaking messages', () => {
    expect(toErrorBody(new NotFoundException('Cannot GET /api/secret'))).toEqual({
      statusCode: 404, code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'No encontrado.',
    });
    expect(toErrorBody(new UnauthorizedException('jwt expired')).code).toBe('UNAUTHENTICATED');
    expect(toErrorBody(new BadRequestException('Unexpected token } in JSON')).code).toBe('VALIDATION_FAILED');
  });

  it('hides unknown errors', () => {
    const body = toErrorBody(new Error('duplicate key value violates unique constraint "users_email_key"'));
    expect(body).toEqual({ statusCode: 500, code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Ocurrió un error inesperado.' });
  });

  it('builds VALIDATION_FAILED details from class-validator errors', () => {
    const error = Object.assign(new ValidationError(), {
      property: 'email', constraints: { isEmail: 'email must be an email' }, children: [],
    });
    const body = toErrorBody(validationExceptionFactory([error]));
    expect(body.code).toBe('VALIDATION_FAILED');
    expect(body.details).toEqual([{ field: 'email', messages: ['email must be an email'] }]);
  });
});
```

- `error-codes.ts`: objeto `ERROR_CODES` con cada fila de "Error codes" (`status` y `message` textuales) `as const satisfies Record<string, { status: number; message: string }>`; `export type ErrorCode = keyof typeof ERROR_CODES;`.
- `app-error.ts`:

```ts
import { HttpException } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ERROR_CODES, type ErrorCode } from './error-codes.js';

export interface ErrorDetail { field: string; messages: string[] }

export class AppError extends HttpException {
  constructor(public readonly code: ErrorCode, details?: ErrorDetail[]) {
    const { status, message } = ERROR_CODES[code];
    super({ statusCode: status, code, message, ...(details ? { details } : {}) }, status);
  }
}
```

- `all-exceptions.filter.ts`: `toErrorBody` (AppError → su `getResponse()`; otra `HttpException` → código por status: 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`, 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`, 403 `FORBIDDEN`, 404 `NOT_FOUND`, resto `INTERNAL_ERROR`, siempre con el mensaje de la tabla; cualquier otra cosa → 500 `INTERNAL_ERROR`); `validationExceptionFactory` (aplana `children` con nombres `parent.child`); `@Catch() class AllExceptionsFilter` que responde `res.status(body.statusCode).json(body)` y, si `statusCode >= 500`, loguea `exception.stack` con `Logger` (sin el cuerpo del request).
- `pagination.ts`: `PaginationQueryDto` con `@IsOptional() @Type(() => Number) @IsInt() @Min(1) page = 1` y `pageSize = 20` con `@Max(100)`.
- `common/auth/*`: decoradores con `SetMetadata` y `createParamDecorator` según "Interfaces".

```ts
});
```

```ts
import { JobRunner } from '../src/common/scheduling/job-runner.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';

describe('JobRunner (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  let runner: JobRunner;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); runner = ctx.app.get(JobRunner); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });

  it('skips when the scheduler is disabled unless forced', async () => {
    expect(await runner.run('test-disabled', async () => ({ n: 1 }))).toEqual({ ran: false, result: {} });
    expect(await runner.run('test-disabled', async () => ({ n: 1 }), { force: true })).toEqual({ ran: true, result: { n: 1 } });
  });

  it('never runs the same job twice at the same time', async () => {
    let release: () => void = () => undefined;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((resolve) => { release = resolve; });
    const first = runner.run('test-lock', async () => { await gate; return { n: 1 }; }, { force: true });
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
    const second = await runner.run('test-lock', async () => ({ n: 2 }), { force: true });
    release();
    expect(second.ran).toBe(false);
    expect((await first).ran).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Implementar app setup, health, JobRunner y util de test**

- `app.setup.ts`:

```ts
import { ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
import type { ConfigType } from '@nestjs/config';
import type { NestExpressApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-express';
import { AllExceptionsFilter, validationExceptionFactory } from './common/errors/all-exceptions.filter.js';
import { appConfig } from './config/app.config.js';

export function configureApp(app: NestExpressApplication): void {
  const config = app.get<ConfigType<typeof appConfig>>(appConfig.KEY);
  app.setGlobalPrefix('api');
  app.enableCors({ origin: config.webUrl, credentials: true });
  // Body parsers go after any raw handlers (Better Auth is mounted before this line in T-005).
  app.useBodyParser('json', { limit: '1mb' });
  app.useBodyParser('urlencoded', { extended: true, limit: '1mb' });
  app.useGlobalPipes(
    new ValidationPipe({ whitelist: true, forbidNonWhitelisted: true, transform: true, exceptionFactory: validationExceptionFactory }),
  );
  app.useGlobalFilters(new AllExceptionsFilter());
  app.enableShutdownHooks();
}
```

- `main.ts`: `NestFactory.create<NestExpressApplication>(AppModule, { bodyParser: false })`, `configureApp(app)`, `app.listen(config.port)`.
- `job-runner.service.ts`: un `QueryRunner` dedicado; `SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext($1)) AS locked`; si no obtuvo el lock, `{ ran: false, result: {} }`; si sí, ejecuta, loguea `job <nombre> <result JSON>` y libera con `pg_advisory_unlock` en un `finally`, y libera el runner en otro `finally`.

```ts
import type { NestExpressApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-express';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { AppModule } from '../../src/app.module.js';
import { configureApp } from '../../src/app.setup.js';

export interface TestApp { app: NestExpressApplication; dataSource: DataSource }

export async function createTestApp(): Promise<TestApp> {
  const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] }).compile();
  const app = moduleRef.createNestApplication<NestExpressApplication>({ bodyParser: false });
  configureApp(app);
  await app.init();
  return { app, dataSource: app.get(DataSource) };
}
```

### Task T-002: Esquema de datos, settings y seed
**Model:** opus · **Depends on:** T-000 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-001, T-003, T-004 · **Contracts:** Data contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** decisions/ADR modulos simples por dominio, constraints/Esquema solo via migraciones, constraints/Nombres snake_case en DB, constraints/Soft delete en datos de negocio, constraints/Reserva distinta de asistencia, constraints/Configuracion operativa administrada por staff, constraints/Accesos y definiciones operativas al inicio

**Files:**
- Create: las 18 entidades de T-002 listadas en "Data contract › Entidades y archivos"
- Create: `apps/api/src/database/entities.ts`, `apps/api/src/database/seed-data.ts`, `apps/api/src/database/seed.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/database/migrations/<timestamp>-InitialSchema.ts` (generada), `apps/api/src/database/migrations/<timestamp>-DefaultSettings.ts` (creada con `migration:create`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/settings/settings.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/settings/settings.defaults.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/settings/models/settings.model.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/settings/services/settings.service.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/utils/db.ts`, `apps/api/test/schema.e2e-spec.ts`, `apps/api/test/settings-service.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/database/database.module.ts` (agregar `entities: ALL_ENTITIES`), `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `SettingsModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `buildDataSourceOptions`, `databaseConfig`, `SnakeNamingStrategy`.
- Produces:
  - Clases de entidad con los nombres del "Data contract"; cada FK expone la columna (`@Column('uuid') userId: string`) y la relación (`@ManyToOne(() => User) @JoinColumn({ name: 'user_id' }) user: User`).
  - `ALL_ENTITIES` (`src/database/entities.ts`).
  - `type SettingsValues` (claves camelCase de "Settings keys"), `SETTINGS_DEFAULTS: SettingsValues`, `SETTING_DB_KEYS: Record<keyof SettingsValues, string>`.
  - `SettingsService.getAll(): Promise<SettingsValues>` (lee la tabla; si falta una clave usa el default), `get<K extends keyof SettingsValues>(key: K): Promise<SettingsValues[K]>`, `update(patch: Partial<SettingsValues>, updatedBy: string): Promise<SettingsValues>` (upsert; lanza `InvalidSettingsError` —clase exportada por el mismo archivo del service— si `prolongedInactivityDays <= noAttendanceDays` en el resultado combinado; T-009 la traduce a `AppError('SETTINGS_INVALID')`). `SettingsModule` exporta `SettingsService`.
  - `seedCatalogAndSchedule(dataSource: DataSource): Promise<{ plans: number; professionals: number; templates: number }>` y `insertDefaultSettings(dataSource: DataSource): Promise<void>` (`src/database/seed-data.ts`).
  - `resetDb(dataSource: DataSource): Promise<void>` en `test/utils/db.ts`: trunca **todas** las tablas del schema `public` salvo `migrations` (lista dinámica de `pg_tables`, `RESTART IDENTITY CASCADE`) y vuelve a insertar los settings default.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `migration:run` sobre una base vacía crea exactamente las tablas, columnas, defaults, CHECKs e índices del "Data contract" (salvo `email_logs`), con los nombres de constraint indicados.
2. La tabla `settings` queda con las 12 claves y sus defaults después de migrar.
3. El índice parcial de `bookings` rechaza una segunda reserva activa del mismo usuario en el mismo turno y la permite si la primera está `cancelled`.
4. Los CHECKs de `plans` rechazan un `period_plan` sin `period_months` o con servicio distinto de `performance`.
5. `seed` es idempotente: correrlo dos veces deja 7 planes, 1 profesional y 100 plantillas (40 Performance, 30 Recovery, 30 Rehab).
6. `resetDb` deja todas las tablas vacías salvo `settings` (con defaults) y `migrations`.
7. Una columna `date` se lee como string `YYYY-MM-DD`.

```ts
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { databaseConfig } from '../src/config/database.config.js';
import { ALL_ENTITIES } from '../src/database/entities.js';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { buildDataSourceOptions } from '../src/database/typeorm.options.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('schema (e2e)', () => {
  let ds: DataSource;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    ds = new DataSource({ ...buildDataSourceOptions(databaseConfig()), entities: ALL_ENTITIES, migrations: [] });
    await ds.initialize();
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await ds.destroy(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ds); });

  const insertUser = async (email: string): Promise<string> => {
    const [row] = await ds.query(`INSERT INTO users (name, email) VALUES ('Ana', $1) RETURNING id`, [email]);
    return row.id as string;
  };

  it('has default settings after reset', async () => {
    const rows: { key: string }[] = await ds.query('SELECT key FROM settings ORDER BY key');
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(12);
  });

  it('rejects a second active booking for the same user and slot', async () => {
    const userId = await insertUser('a@test.move');
    const [slot] = await ds.query(
      `INSERT INTO time_slots (service, starts_at, ends_at, capacity) VALUES ('performance', now() + interval '1 day', now() + interval '1 day 1 hour', 14) RETURNING id`,
    );
    const insert = (status: string) =>
      ds.query(`INSERT INTO bookings (user_id, time_slot_id, status, source) VALUES ($1, $2, $3, 'plan')`, [userId, slot.id, status]);
    await insert('confirmed');
    await expect(insert('confirmed')).rejects.toThrow(/UQ_bookings_active_user_slot/);
    await ds.query(`UPDATE bookings SET status = 'cancelled'`);
    await expect(insert('confirmed')).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

  it('enforces plan shape checks', async () => {
    await expect(
      ds.query(`INSERT INTO plans (service, kind, slug, name, price) VALUES ('performance', 'period_plan', 'x', 'X', 100)`),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
    await expect(
      ds.query(`INSERT INTO plans (service, kind, slug, name, price, period_months) VALUES ('recovery', 'period_plan', 'y', 'Y', 100, 1)`),
    ).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  it('reads date columns as business date strings', async () => {
    const userId = await insertUser('b@test.move');
    await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ds);
    const [plan] = await ds.query(`SELECT id FROM plans WHERE slug = 'performance-mensual'`);
    const [row] = await ds.query(
      `INSERT INTO subscriptions (user_id, plan_id, service, starts_on, ends_on) VALUES ($1, $2, 'performance', '2026-10-12', '2026-11-12') RETURNING starts_on`,
      [userId, plan.id],
    );
    expect(row.starts_on).toBe('2026-10-12');
  });

  it('seeds idempotently', async () => {
    await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ds);
    await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ds);
    const [{ count: plans }] = await ds.query('SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM plans');
    const [{ count: templates }] = await ds.query('SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM schedule_templates');
    const [{ count: pros }] = await ds.query('SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM professionals');
    expect([plans, templates, pros]).toEqual([7, 100, 1]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Entidades**

Escribir las 18 entidades exactamente como el "Data contract" (tabla explícita en `@Entity('<tabla>')`, CHECKs con `@Check('<expresión>')`, índices con `@Index`, `type: 'timestamptz'` en instantes, `type: 'date'` tipado `string` en fechas, `text[]` para `features`, `jsonb` tipado para `context` y `value`). Ejemplo de referencia (`bookings/entities/booking.entity.ts`):

```ts
import {
  Check, Column, CreateDateColumn, DeleteDateColumn, Entity, Index, JoinColumn, ManyToOne,
  PrimaryGeneratedColumn, UpdateDateColumn,
} from 'typeorm';
import { TimeSlot } from '../../schedule/entities/time-slot.entity.js';
import { Subscription } from '../../subscriptions/entities/subscription.entity.js';
import { User } from '../../users/entities/user.entity.js';

export type BookingStatus = 'pending_payment' | 'confirmed' | 'cancelled' | 'expired';
export type BookingSource = 'plan' | 'package' | 'payment' | 'followup';

@Entity('bookings')
@Check(`"status" IN ('pending_payment','confirmed','cancelled','expired')`)
@Check(`"source" IN ('plan','package','payment','followup')`)
@Check(`"cancelled_by" IS NULL OR "cancelled_by" IN ('client','staff','system')`)
@Index('UQ_bookings_active_user_slot', ['userId', 'timeSlotId'], {
  unique: true,
  where: `"status" IN ('pending_payment','confirmed') AND "deleted_at" IS NULL`,
})
@Index(['timeSlotId', 'status'])
@Index(['userId', 'status'])
export class Booking {
  @PrimaryGeneratedColumn('uuid') id: string;
  @Column('uuid') userId: string;
  @ManyToOne(() => User) @JoinColumn({ name: 'user_id' }) user: User;
  @Column('uuid') timeSlotId: string;
  @ManyToOne(() => TimeSlot) @JoinColumn({ name: 'time_slot_id' }) timeSlot: TimeSlot;
  @Column('text') status: BookingStatus;
  @Column('text') source: BookingSource;
  @Column({ type: 'uuid', nullable: true }) subscriptionId: string | null;
  @ManyToOne(() => Subscription, { nullable: true }) @JoinColumn({ name: 'subscription_id' }) subscription: Subscription | null;
  @Column({ type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true }) holdExpiresAt: Date | null;
  @Column({ type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true }) confirmedAt: Date | null;
  @Column({ type: 'timestamptz', nullable: true }) cancelledAt: Date | null;
  @Column({ type: 'text', nullable: true }) cancelledBy: 'client' | 'staff' | 'system' | null;
  @CreateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' }) createdAt: Date;
  @UpdateDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' }) updatedAt: Date;
  @DeleteDateColumn({ type: 'timestamptz' }) deletedAt: Date | null;
}
```

Las tablas de Better Auth (`AuthSession`, `AuthAccount`, `AuthVerification`) también son entidades, para que la migración generada las incluya. `src/database/entities.ts` exporta `ALL_ENTITIES` con las 18 clases. `database.module.ts` agrega `entities: ALL_ENTITIES` al objeto del factory.

```ts
import { ConfigModule } from '@nestjs/config';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { databaseConfig, type DatabaseConfig } from '../src/config/database.config.js';
import { ALL_ENTITIES } from '../src/database/entities.js';
import { buildDataSourceOptions } from '../src/database/typeorm.options.js';
import { InvalidSettingsError, SettingsService } from '../src/modules/settings/services/settings.service.js';
import { SettingsModule } from '../src/modules/settings/settings.module.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('SettingsService (e2e)', () => {
  let ds: DataSource;
  let settings: SettingsService;
  let close: () => Promise<void>;

  beforeAll(async () => {
    const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: [
        ConfigModule.forRoot({ isGlobal: true, load: [databaseConfig] }),
        TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
          inject: [databaseConfig.KEY],
          useFactory: (c: DatabaseConfig) => ({ ...buildDataSourceOptions(c), entities: ALL_ENTITIES }),
        }),
        SettingsModule,
      ],
    }).compile();
    const app = moduleRef.createNestApplication();
    await app.init();
    ds = app.get(DataSource);
    settings = app.get(SettingsService);
    close = () => app.close();
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ds); });

  it('reads defaults', async () => {
    expect(await settings.getAll()).toMatchObject({ cancelLimitHours: 2, bookingWindowDays: 14, valuesProvisional: true });
  });

  it('updates and rejects inconsistent thresholds', async () => {
    const [{ id }] = await ds.query(`INSERT INTO users (name, email, role) VALUES ('S', 's@test.move', 'staff') RETURNING id`);
    expect((await settings.update({ cancelLimitHours: 4 }, id)).cancelLimitHours).toBe(4);
    await expect(settings.update({ prolongedInactivityDays: 5 }, id)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(InvalidSettingsError);
  });
});
```

**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-000 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-001, T-002, T-004 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** concepts/Sistema visual MOVE, constraints/Estilos via tokens del tema, decisions/ADR capa de datos del frontend, constraints/Formularios validados con Zod

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/src/app/globals.css`, `apps/web/src/app/layout.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/providers.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/api/types.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/api/errors.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/api/config.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/api/client.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/api/client.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/api/server.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/format.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth-client.ts`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/move/status-badge.tsx`, `eyebrow.tsx`, `display-title.tsx`, `page-container.tsx`, `plan-card.tsx`, `empty-state.tsx`, `error-state.tsx`, `staff-page-header.tsx`, `status-badge.test.tsx`, `plan-card.test.tsx` (todos en `apps/web/src/components/move/`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: componentes shadcn instalados por T-000 en `@/components/ui/*`.
- Produces:
  - `src/lib/api/types.ts`: el bloque "Types" del HTTP contract, textual.
  - `class ApiError extends Error { status: number; code: ErrorCode | 'NETWORK_ERROR'; details?: ApiErrorBody['details'] }`; `errorMessage(error: unknown): string` (mensaje de la tabla "Error codes" según `code`, o "No pudimos conectar con MOVE. Probá de nuevo." para red).
  - `API_URL` (`process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`, termina en `/api`) y `apiOrigin()` (sin `/api`).
  - `apiFetch<T>(path: string, init?: { method?: string; body?: unknown; query?: Record<string, string | number | boolean | undefined> }): Promise<T>` — browser, `credentials: 'include'`, JSON, lanza `ApiError`; 204 → `undefined as T`.
  - `serverApiFetch<T>(path, init?)` — servidor: reenvía el header `cookie` de `next/headers` y usa `cache: 'no-store'`; `serverApiFetchOrNull<T>(path)` devuelve `null` ante 401.
  - `format.ts`: `formatUYU(n): string` (`$ 3.900`), `formatDate(d: 'YYYY-MM-DD' | ISO): string` (`15/11/2026`), `formatDayMonth(d)` (`15/11`), `formatTime(iso): string` (`19:00`), `formatWeekdayShort(d)` (`Lun`), `formatWeekdayLong(d)` (`Lunes`), `formatDateLong(d)` (`Martes 13/10/2026`), `initials(name)` (R15), `businessTodayWeb(now?: Date): string`, `addDaysWeb(date, n): string`, `SERVICE_LABEL: Record<Service, string>` (R15). Todo en `America/Montevideo`.
  - `authClient` (`createAuthClient` de `better-auth/react` con `baseURL: apiOrigin()` y `basePath: '/api/auth'`).
  - `Providers` (QueryClientProvider con `staleTime: 30_000`, `retry: 1`; `<Toaster />` de sonner).
  - Componentes: `StatusBadge({ tone: 'success' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'neutral'; children })`, `Eyebrow({ index?: string; children })` (renderiza `01 — Servicios`), `DisplayTitle({ as?: 'h1' | 'h2' | 'h3'; size?: 'hero' | 'xl' | 'lg'; children })`, `PageContainer({ children, className? })` (max-w 1160px, px-6), `PlanCard({ plan: PlanDto; ctaHref: string; ctaLabel: string; unitLabel?: string })` (precio `formatUYU`, unidad `/mes`, `/trim`, `/sem`, `/sesión` según kind y period, tag "Más elegido" o "Recomendado" si `featured`, borde blanco si destacado), `EmptyState({ title; description?; action? })`, `ErrorState({ error: unknown; onRetry? })`, `StaffPageHeader({ title; actions? })`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. El tema es oscuro puro con los tokens del sistema visual (`--background #000`, `--soft #0c0c0c`, `--card #121212`, `--border #262626`, `--muted-foreground #9a9a9a`, primario blanco con texto negro, radio 4px) más los tonos `success`, `warning` y `danger` (texto 400 y fondo 10 %), y las fuentes Archivo (`font-display`, 400–900) e Inter (`font-sans`). `html lang="es"`.
2. `apiFetch` envía cookies, serializa JSON, arma query strings, devuelve `undefined` en 204 y convierte cuerpos `ApiErrorBody` en `ApiError` con `code`.
3. Los formatos coinciden con los mockups (`$ 3.900`, `$ 10.500`, `15/11/2026`, `19:00` para `2026-10-12T22:00:00.000Z`).
4. `PlanCard` muestra el tag de destacado, las features y el CTA con el href recibido; nunca muestra "Prioridad de horarios".

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { formatDate, formatDateLong, formatDayMonth, formatTime, formatUYU, initials, businessTodayWeb } from './format';

describe('format', () => {
  it('formats UYU like the mockups', () => {
    expect(formatUYU(3900)).toBe('$ 3.900');
    expect(formatUYU(10500)).toBe('$ 10.500');
    expect(formatUYU(900)).toBe('$ 900');
  });

  it('formats dates and times in Montevideo', () => {
    expect(formatDate('2026-11-15')).toBe('15/11/2026');
    expect(formatDayMonth('2026-11-15')).toBe('15/11');
    expect(formatTime('2026-10-12T22:00:00.000Z')).toBe('19:00');
    expect(formatDateLong('2026-10-13')).toBe('Martes 13/10/2026');
    expect(businessTodayWeb(new Date('2026-10-13T02:00:00.000Z'))).toBe('2026-10-12');
  });

  it('builds initials', () => {
    expect(initials('Martina Suárez')).toBe('MS');
    expect(initials('diego')).toBe('D');
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/lib/api/client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { apiFetch } from './client';
import { ApiError } from './errors';

const mockFetch = (status: number, body?: unknown) =>
  vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(
    new Response(body === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }),
  );

describe('apiFetch', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());

  it('sends credentials, JSON and query', async () => {
    const spy = mockFetch(200, { ok: true });
    await apiFetch('/bookings', { method: 'POST', body: { timeSlotId: 'x' }, query: { scope: 'upcoming', skip: undefined } });
    const [url, init] = spy.mock.calls[0];
    expect(String(url)).toMatch(/\/api\/bookings\?scope=upcoming$/);
    expect(init).toMatchObject({ method: 'POST', credentials: 'include', body: JSON.stringify({ timeSlotId: 'x' }) });
  });

  it('throws ApiError with the API code', async () => {
    mockFetch(409, { statusCode: 409, code: 'SLOT_FULL', message: 'El turno está completo.' });
    await expect(apiFetch('/bookings', { method: 'POST', body: {} })).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'SLOT_FULL' });
  });

  it('returns undefined on 204', async () => {
    mockFetch(204);
    await expect(apiFetch('/staff/plans/1', { method: 'DELETE' })).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('wraps network failures', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockRejectedValue(new TypeError('fetch failed'));
    await expect(apiFetch('/health')).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
  });
});
```

### Task T-004: Notificaciones por email (Brevo)
**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-000 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-001, T-002, T-003 · **Contracts:** Email contract, Data contract · **Vault:** decisions/ADR Brevo para emails, systems/Modulo notificaciones, constraints/Integraciones externas encapsuladas en un servicio, constraints/Jobs programados idempotentes, failures/Alertas o emails duplicados

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/config/brevo.config.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/notifications.module.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/entities/email-log.entity.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/services/notifications.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/services/brevo.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/services/brevo.service.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/templates/index.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/templates/layout.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/notifications/templates/templates.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/database/migrations/<timestamp>-CreateEmailLogs.ts` (generada)
- Create: `apps/api/test/notifications.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `NotificationsModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `appConfig` (`webUrl`), `databaseConfig`.
- Produces:
  - `brevoConfig` (`registerAs('brevo', …)`: `{ apiKey: string | null; senderEmail: string | null; senderName: string; dryRun: boolean }`, `dryRun = !apiKey`).
  - Tipos `EmailTemplate`, `TemplateParams`, `SendEmailInput`, `SendResult` (Email contract) exportados desde `templates/index.ts`; `renderEmail<K extends EmailTemplate>(template: K, params: TemplateParams[K]): { subject: string; html: string; text: string }`.
  - `BrevoService.sendTransactional(input: { to: { email: string; name: string }; subject: string; html: string; text: string }): Promise<{ messageId: string }>` (lanza `BrevoError { status: number | 'network' }`).
  - `NotificationsService.send<K extends EmailTemplate>(input: SendEmailInput<K>): Promise<SendResult>`.
  - `NotificationsModule` (`imports: [ConfigModule.forFeature(brevoConfig), TypeOrmModule.forFeature([EmailLog])]`, exporta `NotificationsService`).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. Una `idempotencyKey` ya usada devuelve `'duplicate'` y no llama a Brevo.
2. Sin `BREVO_API_KEY` (no producción) el email queda `skipped` en `email_logs` y no se hace ninguna llamada HTTP.
3. Con API key: `POST https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email` con header `api-key`, remitente configurado, `htmlContent` y `textContent`; con 2xx queda `sent` con `provider_message_id`; con error queda `failed` con `error_code` y `send` no lanza.
4. Los parámetros se escapan en HTML (`<script>` → `&lt;script&gt;`) y cada template tiene el asunto del Email contract.
5. Los logs no contienen el email del destinatario.

```ts
import { BrevoService } from './brevo.service.js';

describe('BrevoService', () => {
  afterEach(() => vi.restoreAllMocks());
  const service = new BrevoService({ apiKey: 'k', senderEmail: 'hola@example.com', senderName: 'MOVE', dryRun: false });

  it('posts to the Brevo transactional endpoint', async () => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(new Response(JSON.stringify({ messageId: '<m1>' }), { status: 201 }));
    const result = await service.sendTransactional({ to: { email: 'a@b.c', name: 'A' }, subject: 's', html: '<p>h</p>', text: 't' });
    expect(result).toEqual({ messageId: '<m1>' });
    const [url, init] = spy.mock.calls[0];
    expect(url).toBe('https://api.brevo.com/v3/smtp/email');
    expect((init?.headers as Record<string, string>)['api-key']).toBe('k');
    expect(JSON.parse(String(init?.body))).toMatchObject({ sender: { email: 'hola@example.com', name: 'MOVE' }, to: [{ email: 'a@b.c', name: 'A' }] });
  });

  it('throws BrevoError with the HTTP status', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch').mockResolvedValue(new Response('{}', { status: 401 }));
    await expect(service.sendTransactional({ to: { email: 'a@b.c', name: 'A' }, subject: 's', html: 'h', text: 't' }))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { ConfigModule } from '@nestjs/config';
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing';
import { TypeOrmModule } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { DataSource } from 'typeorm';
import { appConfig } from '../src/config/app.config.js';

  let ds: DataSource;
  let close: () => Promise<void>;

    const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({
      imports: [
        ConfigModule.forRoot({ isGlobal: true, load: [appConfig, databaseConfig] }),
        TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
          inject: [databaseConfig.KEY],
          useFactory: (c: DatabaseConfig) => ({ ...buildDataSourceOptions(c), autoLoadEntities: true }),

      ],
    }).compile();
    const app = moduleRef.createNestApplication();
    await app.init();
    service = app.get(NotificationsService);
    ds = app.get(DataSource);

  afterAll(async () => { await close(); });

  const input = {
    template: 'checkin-invite' as const, to: { email: 'ana@test.move', name: 'Ana' }, userId: null,

  };

  it('skips sending without API key and logs it', async () => {
    const fetchSpy = vi.spyOn(globalThis, 'fetch');
    expect(await service.send(input)).toBe('skipped');
    expect(fetchSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const [row] = await ds.query(`SELECT status, template FROM email_logs WHERE idempotency_key = 'checkin:abc'`);
    expect(row).toEqual({ status: 'skipped', template: 'checkin-invite' });
  });

  it('never sends the same key twice', async () => {
    await service.send(input);
    expect(await service.send(input)).toBe('duplicate');
    const [{ count }] = await ds.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM email_logs`);
    expect(count).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

### Task T-005: Autenticación (Better Auth en Nest), usuarios y guard de roles
**Model:** opus · **Depends on:** T-001, T-002, T-004 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-006, T-007, T-008 · **Contracts:** Data contract, HTTP contract, Email contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** decisions/ADR Better Auth con Google OAuth, decisions/ADR ubicacion de Better Auth, constraints/Autorizacion validada en la API, constraints/Una cuenta por email, constraints/Roles persistidos cliente y staff, constraints/Web y API bajo el mismo dominio raiz, failures/Cliente accede a funciones de staff, failures/Cuentas duplicadas por email, constraints/Cuenta se crea durante la reserva

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/users/users.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/users/services/users.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/users/controllers/me.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/users/dto/update-me.dto.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/users/models/user.model.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/auth.constants.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/better-auth.factory.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/better-auth.factory.spec.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/mount-auth.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/services/auth.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/guards/auth.guard.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/auth/guards/auth.guard.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/cli/create-staff.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/utils/auth.ts`, `apps/api/test/auth.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.setup.ts` (montar Better Auth antes de los body parsers), `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `UsersModule`, `AuthModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AppError`, `Public`, `Roles`, `CurrentUser`, `AuthUser`, `RequestWithUser`, `IS_PUBLIC_KEY`, `ROLES_KEY` (T-001); `authConfig`, `appConfig`, `databaseConfig` (T-001); entidades `User`, `AuthAccount`, `AuthVerification` (T-002); `NotificationsService.send` (T-004); `createTestApp` (T-001), `resetDb` (T-002).
- Produces:
  - `UserModel { id: string; name: string; email: string; role: Role; phone: string | null; rehabFollowupEnabled: boolean; createdAt: Date }`.
  - `UsersService`: `findById(id): Promise<UserModel | null>` (excluye borrados), `findByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<UserModel[]>`, `findByEmail(email): Promise<UserModel | null>` (insensible a mayúsculas), `listClients(): Promise<UserModel[]>` (role `client`, sin borrar, orden nombre), `updateProfile(id, patch: { name?: string; phone?: string | null }): Promise<UserModel>`, `setRehabFollowupEnabled(id: string, enabled: boolean, manager?: EntityManager): Promise<UserModel>` (lanza `AppError('USER_NOT_FOUND')`), `toMeDto(user: UserModel): MeDto`.
  - `BETTER_AUTH` (token de inyección) y `type BetterAuthInstance = ReturnType<typeof createBetterAuth>`.
  - `createBetterAuth(deps: { pool: Pool; secret: string; baseURL: string; webUrl: string; cookieDomain: string | null; google: { clientId: string; clientSecret: string } | null; isProduction: boolean; sendResetPassword: (data: { user: { id: string; email: string; name: string }; url: string; token: string }) => Promise<void> })`.
  - `AuthService.createInvitedUser(input: { name: string; email: string; phone?: string | null }): Promise<{ userId: string }>` (lanza `AppError('EMAIL_ALREADY_REGISTERED')`), `AuthService.emailExists(email): Promise<boolean>`.
  - `AuthGuard` global (`APP_GUARD` en `AuthModule`): completa `req.user` (rol leído de `users`, no de la sesión cacheada) también en rutas `@Public()`; 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED` sin sesión; 403 `FORBIDDEN` si `@Roles` no coincide; usuario con `deleted_at` = sin sesión.
  - `mountAuth(app: NestExpressApplication): void` — registra `toNodeHandler(auth)` en `/api/auth/{*splat}` sobre la instancia de Express antes de `useBodyParser`.
  - Helpers de test (`test/utils/auth.ts`): `signUp(app, input?: { name?: string; email?: string; password?: string }): Promise<{ agent: TestAgent; userId: string; email: string }>`, `signUpClient(app)`, `signUpStaff(app, dataSource)` (sign-up y `UPDATE users SET role = 'staff'`), `TEST_ORIGIN` (= `WEB_URL`). Todos los requests de auth mandan el header `Origin: TEST_ORIGIN`.
  - `GET /api/me`, `PATCH /api/me` (HTTP contract).
  - CLI `pnpm --filter @move/api user:create-staff -- --email <e> --name <n> --password <p>`: crea el usuario por `auth.api.signUpEmail` (o lo encuentra si existe) y lo pasa a `staff`; imprime solo el id.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. Registro por email, ingreso, sesión persistente (cookie) y salida funcionan contra `/api/auth/*`; `GET /api/me` devuelve `MeDto` con `role: 'client'` para un registro nuevo (el registro no puede elegir rol).
2. Sin sesión, `GET /api/me` → 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; una ruta `@Roles('staff')` con sesión de cliente → 403 `FORBIDDEN`.
3. Una cuenta por email: registrar `Ana@Test.move` y luego `ana@test.move` falla y deja un solo usuario. Con Google configurado, el login con Google se vincula a la cuenta existente (`accountLinking` con `trustedProviders: ['google']`).
4. `createInvitedUser` crea un cliente sin contraseña y registra un email `account-invitation` (dry-run); con el token de la verificación, `POST /api/auth/reset-password` define la contraseña y luego se puede ingresar.
5. "Olvidé mi contraseña" para un usuario con contraseña registra `password-reset`; la `idempotencyKey` no contiene el token en claro.
6. Las tablas de Better Auth usadas son `users`, `sessions`, `accounts`, `verifications` con columnas snake_case (mapeo del Data contract); los ids son uuid.
7. En `production` las cookies son `Secure`; si `COOKIE_DOMAIN` está definido se usan cookies cross-subdomain con ese dominio.

`apps/api/src/modules/auth/guards/auth.guard.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import type { ExecutionContext } from '@nestjs/common';
import { IS_PUBLIC_KEY } from '../../../common/auth/public.decorator.js';
import { ROLES_KEY } from '../../../common/auth/roles.decorator.js';
import { AuthGuard } from './auth.guard.js';

const makeContext = (req: Record<string, unknown>, meta: Record<string, unknown>): ExecutionContext => {
  const handler = () => undefined;
  Reflect.defineMetadata(IS_PUBLIC_KEY, meta[IS_PUBLIC_KEY], handler);
  Reflect.defineMetadata(ROLES_KEY, meta[ROLES_KEY], handler);
  return {
    getHandler: () => handler, getClass: () => class {},
    switchToHttp: () => ({ getRequest: () => req }),
  } as unknown as ExecutionContext;
};

describe('AuthGuard', () => {
  const client = { id: 'u1', name: 'Ana', email: 'a@x', role: 'client', phone: null, rehabFollowupEnabled: false, createdAt: new Date() };
  const makeGuard = (sessionUserId: string | null, user = client) =>
    new AuthGuard(
      new Reflector(),
      { api: { getSession: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(sessionUserId ? { user: { id: sessionUserId } } : null) } } as never,
      { findById: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(sessionUserId ? user : null) } as never,
    );

  it('rejects anonymous requests on protected routes', async () => {
    await expect(makeGuard(null).canActivate(makeContext({ headers: {} }, {}))).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED' });
  });

  it('allows anonymous requests on public routes', async () => {
    await expect(makeGuard(null).canActivate(makeContext({ headers: {} }, { [IS_PUBLIC_KEY]: true }))).resolves.toBe(true);
  });

  it('attaches the user on public routes when there is a session', async () => {
    const req: Record<string, unknown> = { headers: {} };
    await makeGuard('u1').canActivate(makeContext(req, { [IS_PUBLIC_KEY]: true }));
    expect(req.user).toEqual({ id: 'u1', name: 'Ana', email: 'a@x', role: 'client' });
  });

  it('forbids clients on staff routes', async () => {
    await expect(makeGuard('u1').canActivate(makeContext({ headers: {} }, { [ROLES_KEY]: ['staff'] })))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
  });
});
```

- `users.service.ts`: repositorio `User`; `findByEmail` con `LOWER(email) = LOWER($1)`; `toMeDto`.
- `better-auth.factory.ts`: `betterAuth({ database: deps.pool, secret, baseURL: deps.baseURL, basePath: '/api/auth', trustedOrigins: [deps.webUrl], emailAndPassword: { enabled: true, minPasswordLength: 8, requireEmailVerification: false, sendResetPassword: deps.sendResetPassword, resetPasswordTokenExpiresIn: 3600 }, socialProviders: deps.google ? { google: deps.google } : {}, user / session / account / verification` con el mapeo del Data contract, `account.accountLinking: { enabled: true, trustedProviders: ['google'] }`, `advanced: { database: { generateId: 'uuid' }, useSecureCookies: deps.isProduction, crossSubDomainCookies: deps.cookieDomain ? { enabled: true, domain: deps.cookieDomain } : undefined } })`.
- `auth.module.ts`: provider `BETTER_AUTH` con factory que crea un `pg.Pool` desde `databaseConfig` (`ssl` según config) y llama `createBetterAuth`; `sendResetPassword` delega en `AuthService.sendPasswordEmail`; cierra el pool en `onModuleDestroy`. Providers: `AuthService`, `{ provide: APP_GUARD, useClass: AuthGuard }`. Imports: `UsersModule`, `NotificationsModule`, `TypeOrmModule.forFeature([AuthAccount, AuthVerification])`.
- `auth.service.ts`:
  - `sendPasswordEmail({ user, url, token })`: si el usuario tiene fila en `accounts` con `provider_id = 'credential'` → template `password-reset`, si no `account-invitation`; `idempotencyKey` = `<template>:<16 hex de sha256(token)>`; params `{ name, url }`.

- `auth.guard.ts`: `auth.api.getSession({ headers: fromNodeHeaders(req.headers) })` → `UsersService.findById(session.user.id)` → `req.user = { id, email, name, role }`; reglas de "Interfaces".
- `mount-auth.ts`: `const auth = app.get<BetterAuthInstance>(BETTER_AUTH); app.getHttpAdapter().getInstance().all('/api/auth/{*splat}', toNodeHandler(auth));`.
- `app.setup.ts`: llamar `mountAuth(app)` justo antes de `app.useBodyParser('json', …)`.
- `me.controller.ts`: `@Controller('me')` con `GET` y `PATCH` (`UpdateMeDto`: `@IsOptional() @IsString() @Length(2, 80) name`, `@IsOptional() @ValidateIf(v => v !== null) @IsString() @Length(6, 20) phone`).
- `create-staff.ts`: parsea `--email`, `--name`, `--password` de `process.argv`; arma una app Nest con `NestFactory.createApplicationContext(AppModule)`; si no existe el usuario llama `auth.api.signUpEmail({ body: { email, name, password } })`; luego `UPDATE users SET role = 'staff'`; imprime `staff <id>`; nunca imprime la contraseña.

```ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { AuthService } from '../src/modules/auth/services/auth.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUp, TEST_ORIGIN } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('auth (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); });
  const http = () => request(ctx.app.getHttpServer());

  it('signs up as client and reads /me', async () => {
    const { agent, email } = await signUp(ctx.app, { name: 'Martina Suárez' });
    const me = await agent.get('/api/me').expect(200);
    expect(me.body).toMatchObject({ name: 'Martina Suárez', email, role: 'client', phone: null, rehabFollowupEnabled: false });
  });

  it('cannot choose role on sign-up', async () => {
    const res = await http().post('/api/auth/sign-up/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN)
      .send({ name: 'X', email: 'x@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!', role: 'staff' });
    const [row] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT role FROM users WHERE email = 'x@test.move'`);
    expect(res.status === 200 ? row.role : 'client').toBe('client');
  });

  it('rejects anonymous /me with the error envelope', async () => {
    const res = await http().get('/api/me').expect(401);
    expect(res.body).toEqual({ statusCode: 401, code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED', message: 'Tenés que iniciar sesión.' });
  });

  it('keeps one account per email (case-insensitive)', async () => {
    await signUp(ctx.app, { email: 'Ana@Test.move' });
    await http().post('/api/auth/sign-up/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN)
      .send({ name: 'Ana 2', email: 'ana@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!' });
    const [{ count }] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM users WHERE LOWER(email) = 'ana@test.move'`);
    expect(count).toBe(1);
  });

  it('signs out', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUp(ctx.app);
    await agent.post('/api/auth/sign-out').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).expect(200);
    await agent.get('/api/me').expect(401);
  });

  it('updates the profile', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUp(ctx.app);
    const res = await agent.patch('/api/me').send({ phone: '099123456' }).expect(200);
    expect(res.body.phone).toBe('099123456');
    await agent.patch('/api/me').send({ role: 'staff' }).expect(400);
  });

  it('invites a user who then sets a password through the reset flow', async () => {
    const { userId } = await ctx.app.get(AuthService).createInvitedUser({ name: 'Lucía Pereira', email: 'lu@test.move' });
    const [log] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT template, user_id, idempotency_key FROM email_logs`);
    expect(log.template).toBe('account-invitation');
    const [verification] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT identifier FROM verifications ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`);
    const token = String(verification.identifier).replace(/^reset-password:/, '');
    expect(log.idempotency_key).not.toContain(token);
    await http().post('/api/auth/reset-password').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).send({ newPassword: 'NuevaClave123!', token }).expect(200);
    const login = request.agent(ctx.app.getHttpServer());
    await login.post('/api/auth/sign-in/email').set('Origin', TEST_ORIGIN).send({ email: 'lu@test.move', password: 'NuevaClave123!' }).expect(200);
    expect((await login.get('/api/me').expect(200)).body.id).toBe(userId);
  });

  it('rejects inviting an existing email', async () => {
    await signUp(ctx.app, { email: 'dup@test.move' });
    await expect(ctx.app.get(AuthService).createInvitedUser({ name: 'D', email: 'DUP@test.move' }))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'EMAIL_ALREADY_REGISTERED' });
  });
});
```

Si el formato del `identifier` de la verificación difiere en la versión instalada, ajustar la extracción del token y anotarlo en el reporte.

### Task T-006: Layouts, navegación y guards de rutas en la web
**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-003 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-005, T-007, T-008 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** systems/Panel staff web, systems/Area cliente web, concepts/Sistema visual MOVE

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(public)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(cliente)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/staff/layout.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/layout/public-header.tsx`, `public-footer.tsx`, `client-header.tsx`, `staff-sidebar.tsx`, `staff-topbar.tsx`, `logout-button.tsx`, `notices-bell.tsx`, `staff-sidebar.test.tsx`, `public-header.test.tsx` (todos en `apps/web/src/components/layout/`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/session.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/session.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/safe-next.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/safe-next.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `serverApiFetchOrNull`, `apiFetch`, `authClient`, tipos (`MeDto`, `NoticeDto`, `AlertCountsDto`), `PageContainer`, `initials` (T-003).
- Produces:
  - `getCurrentUser(): Promise<MeDto | null>` (server; `GET /me`, `null` si 401).
  - `requireRole(role: Role, currentPath: string): Promise<MeDto>` (server; redirige con `redirect()` de `next/navigation` según los guards del route map).
  - `safeNextPath(next: string | null | undefined, role: Role): string | null` (solo rutas internas que empiezan con `/` y no con `//`; `/staff*` solo para staff; rutas cliente solo para client).
  - Layouts de los grupos `(public)`, `(cliente)` y `staff` con la navegación exacta del route map. `StaffSidebar` muestra el badge de alertas (`GET /staff/alerts/counts`, React Query, refetch cada 60 s) y marca el ítem activo por `usePathname()`.
  - `LogoutButton` (`authClient.signOut()` y `router.replace('/')`).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. El layout público muestra navbar sticky (`h-16`, `bg-background/90` con blur), links Inicio/Performance/Rehab/Recovery/Planes, "Ingresar" (o "Mi perfil"/"Panel" según rol), "Reservar", y footer con "© 2026 MOVE Strength & Conditioning", como `01-sitio-publico.html`.
2. `(cliente)` exige sesión de cliente; `staff` exige sesión de staff; los redirects siguen el route map, con `next` = ruta actual.
3. La sidebar staff tiene los 6 ítems (con subitem Check-in), el ítem activo resaltado y el badge con el total de alertas abiertas (oculto si es 0).
4. `safeNextPath` rechaza URLs externas (`https://…`, `//evil.com`) y rutas de otro rol.
5. Lo que lee cookies está dentro de `<Suspense>` (cacheComponents).

Cómo obtener la ruta actual en un layout de servidor: si no hay API directa, cada layout pasa la ruta del grupo y el componente cliente usa `usePathname()`; documentar la elección en el reporte.

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const redirect = vi.fn((url: string) => { throw new Error(`REDIRECT:${url}`); });
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ redirect }));
const serverApiFetchOrNull = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/api/server', () => ({ serverApiFetchOrNull }));

const { requireRole } = await import('./session');

describe('requireRole', () => {
  beforeEach(() => { redirect.mockClear(); serverApiFetchOrNull.mockReset(); });

  it('sends anonymous users to login with next', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue(null);
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/ingresar?next=%2Fperfil');
  });

  it('sends staff away from the client area and clients away from staff', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '1', role: 'staff' });
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/staff');
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '2', role: 'client' });
    await expect(requireRole('staff', '/staff')).rejects.toThrow('REDIRECT:/perfil');
  });

  it('returns the user when the role matches', async () => {
    serverApiFetchOrNull.mockResolvedValue({ id: '2', role: 'client' });
    await expect(requireRole('client', '/perfil')).resolves.toMatchObject({ id: '2' });
  });
});
```

### Task T-008: Páginas de acceso (ingresar, registro, recuperar, restablecer, continuar)
**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-003 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-005, T-006, T-007 · **Contracts:** HTTP contract, Frontend route map · **Vault:** screens/Pantalla acceso, constraints/Cuenta se crea durante la reserva

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/layout.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/ingresar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/registro/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/recuperar/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/restablecer/page.tsx`, `apps/web/src/app/(auth)/continuar/page.tsx`
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/auth/login-form.tsx`, `register-form.tsx`, `forgot-password-form.tsx`, `reset-password-form.tsx`, `google-button.tsx`, `auth-tabs.tsx`, `profiles-info.tsx`, `schemas.ts`, `login-form.test.tsx`, `register-form.test.tsx`, `reset-password-form.test.tsx` (todos en `apps/web/src/components/auth/`)
- Create: `apps/web/src/lib/auth/post-login.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/auth/post-login.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `authClient`, `apiFetch`, `MeDto`, `PageContainer`, `DisplayTitle`, `Eyebrow` (T-003); componentes shadcn `input`, `label`, `button`, `tabs`.
- Produces:
  - `schemas.ts`: `loginSchema` (email, password ≥1), `registerSchema` (name 2–80, email, password ≥8, phone opcional 6–20), `forgotSchema` (email), `resetSchema` (password ≥8, confirm igual).
  - `resolvePostLogin(me: MeDto, next: string | null): string` — misma regla que `safeNextPath` de T-006, reimplementada acá (T-006 es de la misma ola): `next` interno y permitido para el rol, o `/staff` / `/perfil`.
  - Ingreso exitoso (email o Google) → `/continuar?next=<next>`; `/continuar` (cliente) llama `GET /me` y hace `router.replace(resolvePostLogin(me, next))`.
  - Google: botón visible solo si `process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_GOOGLE_ENABLED === 'true'`; `authClient.signIn.social({ provider: 'google', callbackURL: `${window.location.origin}/continuar?next=…` })`.
  - Recuperar: `authClient.requestPasswordReset({ email, redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/restablecer` })` (o el nombre instalado) y siempre el mismo mensaje: "Si el email está registrado, te enviamos un link para restablecer tu contraseña."
  - Restablecer: lee `?token=`; `authClient.resetPassword({ newPassword, token })`; éxito → "Listo, ya podés ingresar" con link a `/ingresar`; sin token o token inválido → mensaje y link a `/recuperar`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `/ingresar` reproduce `02-perfiles-usuario.html`: navbar mínima "← Volver al sitio", "Entrá a MOVE.", tabs "Iniciar sesión" / "Crear cuenta" (esta última navega a `/registro` conservando `next`), card de login, "¿Olvidaste tu contraseña?", divisor "o continuá con", Google (si está habilitado), nota "¿Primera vez en MOVE? Podés reservar Rehab o Recovery sin ser alumno — tu cuenta se crea durante el proceso." y las 3 cards de perfiles.
2. Los formularios validan con Zod antes de enviar y muestran errores por campo en español.
3. Credenciales inválidas muestran "Email o contraseña incorrectos." sin revelar si el email existe; email ya registrado en `/registro` muestra "Ya existe una cuenta con ese email." con link a ingresar.
4. Después de ingresar o registrarse, el usuario llega a su área según rol, o a `next` si es válido para su rol.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { MeDto } from '@/lib/api/types';
import { resolvePostLogin } from './post-login';

const me = (role: MeDto['role']): MeDto => ({ id: '1', name: 'A', email: 'a@x', role, phone: null, rehabFollowupEnabled: false });

describe('resolvePostLogin', () => {
  it('honors a valid next for the role', () => {
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), '/performance')).toBe('/performance');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('staff'), '/staff/turnos')).toBe('/staff/turnos');
  });
  it('falls back to the role home', () => {
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), '/staff')).toBe('/perfil');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('staff'), null)).toBe('/staff');
    expect(resolvePostLogin(me('client'), 'https://evil.com')).toBe('/perfil');
  });
});
```

`apps/web/src/components/auth/login-form.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const push = vi.fn();
vi.mock('next/navigation', () => ({ useRouter: () => ({ push, replace: push }), useSearchParams: () => new URLSearchParams('next=/performance') }));
const signInEmail = vi.fn();
vi.mock('@/lib/auth-client', () => ({ authClient: { signIn: { email: signInEmail } } }));

const { LoginForm } = await import('./login-form');

describe('LoginForm', () => {
  beforeEach(() => { push.mockReset(); signInEmail.mockReset(); });

  it('validates before submitting', async () => {
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Ingresá un email válido.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(signInEmail).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('signs in and continues with next', async () => {
    signInEmail.mockResolvedValue({ data: { user: { id: '1' } }, error: null });
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@test.move');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), 'Secreto123!');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(signInEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: 'ana@test.move', password: 'Secreto123!' });
    expect(push).toHaveBeenCalledWith('/continuar?next=%2Fperformance');
  });

  it('shows a generic error on bad credentials', async () => {
    signInEmail.mockResolvedValue({ data: null, error: { status: 401, message: 'Invalid email or password' } });
    render(<LoginForm />);
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Email'), 'ana@test.move');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Contraseña'), 'mala');
    await userEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ingresar' }));
    expect(await screen.findByText('Email o contraseña incorrectos.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
```

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. `GET /api/catalog/plans?service=performance` devuelve solo planes activos y no borrados de ese servicio, ordenados por `sortOrder`, con la forma `PlanDto`; sin sesión.
2. Staff crea, edita (sin cambiar `service` ni `kind`), desactiva y borra (soft) planes; `slug` duplicado → 409 `SLUG_TAKEN`; combinación inválida → 400 `PLAN_KIND_INVALID`.
3. Un cliente en `/api/staff/plans` recibe 403 `FORBIDDEN`; sin sesión, 401.
4. `GET /api/settings/public` devuelve `{ cancelLimitHours, bookingWindowDays }`; `PATCH /api/staff/settings` valida rangos (DTO) y la regla cruzada (`SETTINGS_INVALID`), y registra `updated_by`.

```ts
import { validatePlanShape } from './plan-rules.js';

const base = { periodMonths: null, periodDays: null, sessionsIncluded: null, durationMinutes: null };

describe('validatePlanShape', () => {
  it('accepts the four valid shapes', () => {
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', periodMonths: 3 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'session_pack', periodDays: 30, sessionsIncluded: 4, durationMinutes: 45 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'single_session', durationMinutes: 45 })).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'rehab', kind: 'evaluation', durationMinutes: 60 })).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('rejects mismatched service or missing fields', () => {

    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan' })).toThrow();
    expect(() => validatePlanShape({ ...base, service: 'recovery', kind: 'session_pack', periodDays: 30 })).toThrow();

});
```

```ts
import request from 'supertest';
import { seedCatalogAndSchedule } from '../src/database/seed-data.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient, signUpStaff } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('catalog (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); await seedCatalogAndSchedule(ctx.dataSource); });

  it('lists active plans publicly in order', async () => {
    const res = await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/catalog/plans?service=performance').expect(200);
    expect(res.body.map((p: { slug: string }) => p.slug)).toEqual(['performance-mensual', 'performance-trimestral', 'performance-semestral']);
    expect(res.body[1]).toMatchObject({ price: 10500, currency: 'UYU', periodMonths: 3, featured: true });
  });

  it('lets staff manage plans and hides inactive ones from the public list', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const created = await agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'performance-anual', name: 'Plan Anual', price: 36000, periodMonths: 12 })
      .expect(201);
    await agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'performance', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'performance-anual', name: 'Dup', price: 1, periodMonths: 1 })
      .expect(409).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('SLUG_TAKEN'));
    await agent.patch(`/api/staff/plans/${created.body.id}`).send({ active: false }).expect(200);
    const pub = await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/catalog/plans?service=performance').expect(200);
    expect(pub.body.some((p: { slug: string }) => p.slug === 'performance-anual')).toBe(false);
    await agent.delete(`/api/staff/plans/${created.body.id}`).expect(204);
  });

  it('rejects invalid shapes and non-staff users', async () => {
    const staff = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    await staff.agent.post('/api/staff/plans')
      .send({ service: 'recovery', kind: 'period_plan', slug: 'bad', name: 'Bad', price: 1, periodMonths: 1 })
      .expect(400).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('PLAN_KIND_INVALID'));
    const client = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await client.agent.get('/api/staff/plans').expect(403);
    await request(ctx.app.getHttpServer()).get('/api/staff/plans').expect(401);
  });
});
```

**Model:** sonnet · **Depends on:** T-005 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-009, T-011, T-012 · **Contracts:** Data contract, HTTP contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** systems/Modulo agenda, decisions/ADR calendario propio, concepts/Horario, concepts/Disponibilidad profesional, constraints/Horario Rehab se bloquea al reservar

- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/controllers/staff-professionals.controller.ts`, `staff-templates.controller.ts`, `staff-slots.controller.ts` (en `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/controllers/`)
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/schedule.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/professionals.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/services/templates.service.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/domain/slot-generation.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/domain/slot-generation.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/jobs/generate-slots.job.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/dto/*.ts` (create/update professional, create/update template, create slot, generate), `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/models/time-slot.model.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/schedule/models/professional.model.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/schedule.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `ScheduleModule.forRoot()` de `@nestjs/schedule` —lo usan todos los crons del plan— y `AgendaModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AppError`, `Roles`, `JobRunner`, helpers de R1 (T-001); entidades `Professional`, `ScheduleTemplate`, `TimeSlot` (T-002); `SettingsService` (T-002).
- Produces:
  - `TimeSlotModel { id: string; service: Service; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; capacity: number; professional: { id: string; name: string } | null; templateId: string | null; cancelledAt: Date | null }`.
  - `ScheduleService` (clase en `services/schedule.service.ts`, exportada por `AgendaModule`):
    - `lockSlot(manager: EntityManager, slotId: string): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` — `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` sobre `time_slots`; lanza `SLOT_NOT_FOUND`.
    - `getSlot(slotId): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` (lanza `SLOT_NOT_FOUND`).
    - `listSlots(opts: { service?: Service; from: Date; to: Date; includeCancelled?: boolean }): Promise<TimeSlotModel[]>` (orden `startsAt`).
    - `cancelSlot(manager, slotId): Promise<TimeSlotModel>`; `updateCapacity(manager, slotId, capacity): Promise<TimeSlotModel>` (rehab: solo 1, si no `TEMPLATE_INVALID`).
    - `createSlot(input: { service; date; startTime; durationMinutes; capacity; professionalId? }): Promise<TimeSlotModel>`.
    - `generateSlots(fromDate: string, days: number): Promise<number>` (idempotente por `UQ_time_slots_template_start`).
  - `buildSlotsFromTemplates(templates: TemplateSpec[], fromDate: string, days: number): SlotSpec[]` (pura) con `TemplateSpec { id; service; weekday; startTime; durationMinutes; capacity; professionalId }` y `SlotSpec { templateId; service; startsAt: Date; endsAt: Date; capacity; professionalId }`.
  - Job `generate-slots`: cron `0 3 * * *` (`timeZone: BUSINESS_TZ`) y una vez en `onApplicationBootstrap`, ambos con `JobRunner.run('generate-slots', …)` y `days = bookingWindowDays + 1` desde `businessToday()`.
  - Endpoints de agenda staff del HTTP contract (owner T-010). `POST /staff/time-slots` responde `StaffTimeSlotDto` con contadores en 0.

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. Las plantillas generan un turno por fecha cuyo `isoWeekday` coincide, con `starts_at = businessDateTime(fecha, startTime)` y `ends_at = starts_at + durationMinutes`, para `[fromDate, fromDate + days)`.
2. Generar dos veces no duplica turnos; desactivar o borrar una plantilla detiene la generación futura sin tocar turnos ya generados; editar una plantilla afecta solo turnos que se generen después.
3. Plantillas y turnos de Rehab exigen profesional y cupo 1 (`TEMPLATE_INVALID`); profesional inexistente → `PROFESSIONAL_NOT_FOUND`.
4. Al crear o editar una plantilla se generan sus turnos dentro de la ventana de reserva.
5. Todos los endpoints son `staff` (403 para clientes).

```ts
  });

  });
});
```

```ts
import { addDays, businessToday } from '../src/common/time/business-time.js';
import { ScheduleService } from '../src/modules/schedule/services/schedule.service.js';
import { createTestApp, type TestApp } from './utils/app.js';
import { signUpClient, signUpStaff } from './utils/auth.js';
import { resetDb } from './utils/db.js';

describe('schedule (e2e)', () => {
  let ctx: TestApp;
  beforeAll(async () => { ctx = await createTestApp(); });
  afterAll(async () => { await ctx.app.close(); });
  beforeEach(async () => { await resetDb(ctx.dataSource); });

  it('creates templates, generates idempotently and validates rehab', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const pro = await agent.post('/api/staff/professionals').send({ name: 'Fisio Uno' }).expect(201);
    await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/templates')
      .send({ service: 'rehab', weekday: 2, startTime: '11:00', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 2, professionalId: pro.body.id })
      .expect(400).expect((r) => expect(r.body.code).toBe('TEMPLATE_INVALID'));
    for (let weekday = 1; weekday <= 7; weekday += 1) {
      await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/templates')
        .send({ service: 'performance', weekday, startTime: '23:59', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 14 }).expect(201);
    }
    const [{ count: first }] = await ctx.dataSource.query(`SELECT count(*)::int AS count FROM time_slots`);
    expect(first).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(14);
    const again = await agent.post('/api/staff/schedule/generate').send({}).expect(201);
    expect(again.body.created).toBe(0);
  });

  it('locks a slot row inside a transaction', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpStaff(ctx.app, ctx.dataSource);
    const slot = await agent.post('/api/staff/time-slots')
      .send({ service: 'performance', date: addDays(businessToday(), 1), startTime: '19:00', durationMinutes: 60, capacity: 14 }).expect(201);
    const schedule = ctx.app.get(ScheduleService);
    const locked = await ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => schedule.lockSlot(m, slot.body.id));
    expect(locked).toMatchObject({ id: slot.body.id, capacity: 14 });
    await expect(ctx.dataSource.transaction((m) => schedule.lockSlot(m, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')))
      .rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'SLOT_NOT_FOUND' });
  });

  it('is staff only', async () => {
    const { agent } = await signUpClient(ctx.app);
    await agent.get('/api/staff/schedule/templates').expect(403);
  });
});
```

### Task T-011: Suscripciones — vigencia, renovación y paquetes Recovery
**Model:** opus · **Depends on:** T-005 integrated · **Parallel with:** T-009, T-010, T-012 · **Contracts:** Data contract, HTTP contract, Domain rules · **Vault:** systems/Modulo suscripciones, concepts/Suscripcion, constraints/Renovacion extiende desde el vencimiento, concepts/Paquete de sesiones Recovery, constraints/Sesiones Recovery no exceden el paquete, concepts/Sesiones Recovery no usadas, failures/Dias de plan perdidos al renovar

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/subscriptions.module.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/controllers/my-subscriptions.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/services/subscriptions.service.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/domain/vigencia.ts`, `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/domain/vigencia.spec.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/src/modules/subscriptions/models/subscription.model.ts`
- Create: `apps/api/test/subscriptions.e2e-spec.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts` (registrar `SubscriptionsModule`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: helpers R1, `AppError`, `Roles`, `CurrentUser` (T-001); entidades `Subscription`, `Plan` (T-002, la relación `plan` se usa para leer `name`); `SettingsService` (`expiringSoonDays`).
- Produces:
  - `SubscriptionModel { id; userId; planId; planName; service: 'performance' | 'recovery'; startsOn: string; endsOn: string; sessionsTotal: number | null; sessionsUsed: number; purchaseId: string | null; createdAt: Date }`.
  - `PlanSnapshot { id: string; name: string; service: Service; kind: PlanKind; periodMonths: number | null; periodDays: number | null; sessionsIncluded: number | null }`.
  - Funciones puras (`domain/vigencia.ts`): `computeActivationPeriod(plan: PlanSnapshot, latestPerformanceEndsOn: string | null, today: string): { startsOn: string; endsOn: string; sessionsTotal: number | null }` (R3); `covers(sub, date): boolean` (R2); `performanceSummary(subs: SubscriptionModel[], today: string): { current; upcoming; latest }` (R4); `toSubscriptionDto(sub, today, expiringSoonDays): SubscriptionDto` (R4); `toPackDto(sub): PackDto`.
  - `SubscriptionsService`:
    - `activateFromPurchase(manager: EntityManager, input: { purchaseId: string; userId: string; plan: PlanSnapshot; today: string }): Promise<SubscriptionModel>` (R3; idempotente por `purchase_id`; lanza `PLAN_KIND_INVALID` si el kind no es `period_plan` ni `session_pack`).
    - `findCoveringPerformance(manager: EntityManager, userId: string, date: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>`.
    - `consumePackSession(manager: EntityManager, userId: string, date: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>` (R5).
    - `releasePackSession(manager: EntityManager, subscriptionId: string): Promise<void>`.
    - `getMySubscriptions(userId: string, today: string): Promise<MySubscriptionsDto>`.
    - `listPerformanceByUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<SubscriptionModel[]>` y `listActivePacksByUsers(userIds: string[], today: string): Promise<SubscriptionModel[]>`.
    - `findById(id): Promise<SubscriptionModel | null>`.
  - `GET /api/me/subscriptions` (client).

**Acceptance criteria:**
1. R3 exacto: compra inicial, renovación anticipada (sin perder días) y renovación después de vencer; paquete de 30 días con su saldo.
2. Activar dos veces la misma compra devuelve la misma suscripción (una sola fila).
3. `findCoveringPerformance` cubre el último día completo ("vence el 15/11" cubre el 15/11) y no el 16/11.
4. Dos consumos concurrentes sobre un paquete con 1 sesión dejan exactamente uno exitoso; con saldo 0 devuelve `null`; `releasePackSession` devuelve la sesión.
5. `GET /api/me/subscriptions` devuelve `MySubscriptionsDto` con `status`, `daysLeft` y `progressPct` según R4.

```ts
import { computeActivationPeriod, covers, toSubscriptionDto } from './vigencia.js';

const monthly = { id: 'p1', name: 'Plan Mensual', service: 'performance' as const, kind: 'period_plan' as const, periodMonths: 1, periodDays: null, sessionsIncluded: null };
const quarterly = { ...monthly, id: 'p3', name: 'Plan Trimestral', periodMonths: 3 };
const pack4 = { id: 'r4', name: 'Paquete 4 sesiones', service: 'recovery' as const, kind: 'session_pack' as const, periodMonths: null, periodDays: 30, sessionsIncluded: 4 };

describe('computeActivationPeriod', () => {
  it('starts today on first purchase', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(quarterly, null, '2026-08-15')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-08-15', endsOn: '2026-11-15', sessionsTotal: null });
  });
  it('extends from the current end when renewing early', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(monthly, '2026-11-15', '2026-11-10')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-11-16', endsOn: '2026-12-15', sessionsTotal: null });
  });
  it('restarts today after expiry', () => {

  });
  it('gives packs 30 days and their sessions', () => {
    expect(computeActivationPeriod(pack4, null, '2026-10-01')).toEqual({ startsOn: '2026-10-01', endsOn: '2026-10-30', sessionsTotal: 4 });
  });
});

describe('covers and DTO', () => {

  it('covers the whole last day', () => {

    expect(covers(sub, '2026-11-16')).toBe(false);
  });

    expect(toSubscriptionDto(sub, '2026-11-10', 7).status).toBe('expiring');
  });
});
```
